## Intake and current activation

`design_reframe` at the TypeScript GTL/HoG selection boundary. Product intent,
catalogue scope, F_D/F_P/F_H authority and execution-conformance requirements
are fixed by the accepted definition. First establish whether existing gates,
action-catalogue declarations and traversal routes already realize the two-choice
relation; a fixed callee at one workflow locus is not alone proof that another
constructor or relaxed guard is required.

Root enters Writer solely for this execution record and T-287 work state, then
returns to Executive. Authorized next work proceeds through separately bounded
Worker grants; reviewers remain independent. No unrelated cleanup, dynamic graph
generation or revival of the withdrawn odd_glc planner is selected.

which rejected the response schema with
`400 tools.1.custom.input_schema.type: Field required` before answering. Its

execution was 4.13 s including a 2.11 s actor interval. No child was launched.

Executive selects a local realization correction: declare `type: object` at
the root of the existing object-only raw-choice union. This is equivalent JSON
Schema meaning and required provider correspondence, not new choice law or a
fallback. The Worker may correct that profile and the fixture's event-name
timing typo, rebuild/package once, then execute the three still-unproved native
cases. Keep both failed attempts and their exact archives/evidence separate and
immutable. No passed native judgment exists to repeat. Root enters Writer only
for this disposition and then returns to Executive; the S3 grant otherwise holds.

Attempt-03 additionally refused top-level `oneOf`/`allOf`/`anyOf` before an
answer. Adding only the root type did not complete provider correspondence;
that first correction was insufficient. Executive stops guessed schema-keyword
iterations. The [provider's structured-output documentation](https://code.claude.com/docs/en/agent-sdk/structured-outputs)
documents root objects, properties and required fields plus application
validation; it does not establish that conditionals repair this path.

Select one closed root-object transport schema: disposition enum and required
reason/evidence, optional domain-constrained graph identity and missing-support
fields with explicit applicability descriptions. Preserve the existing strict
branch validator/binder as the authority for exactly the selected or gap shape.
Thus provider shaping is a necessary structural subset; it never establishes
admissibility alone. Preserve observed bytes and exact observed/result
correspondence; reject branch-inappropriate fields rather than silently removing
them. No fallback, coercion or weakened admitted choice contract is selected.

The existing Worker may clarify that two-layer correspondence in the native
HOW, preserve its preimage, make this profile-only schema correction and normal
generated updates, then run the remaining three cases on one new frozen build.
Include invalid-branch refusal in the already-authorized mechanical negatives.
Retain attempt-03 unchanged. Root's separate Writer scope is this disposition;
then Root returns to Executive. This is recorded as external-interface rework,
not native semantic success or evidence that no integration gap existed.
